## 28. DataFlow

- **Identity:** Shared tenant+id address, foreign sentinels, RequestId creation identity. Semantic asset-list equality for immediate create retry but compares current state after revisions. Expected revision/physical OCC; no selected prior operation recognition.
- **Invariant:** One tenant inventory identity has one record kind; flow endpoint/asset scope and encryption or approved-exception terms must match highest admitted carried classification, and effective date never moves backward.
- **Boundary:** retain. Shared type exclusion must remain in one physical slot. Complete carried-asset relation is evidence for one atomic encryption rule; asset identity/lifecycle remains separate. No reason for all flows to share a revision.
- **Current observations:** ROOTTECH-02 (pending), ROOTTECH-05 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Handler resolves endpoints/assets and passes classification string/value; no live other aggregate enters flow. This is snapshot admission of another owner rule, not perpetual enforcement after asset reclassification.
  - State/frame guard: Current complete content incl asset ID list and highest observed classification, revision/kind flags. Asset list capped100; no stream lifetime bound because unlimited revisions append full lists.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `RecordDataFlow` → RecordDataFlowHandler; targets `DataFlow`; scope `IRequest<DataFlowRegistration>, ITechnologyInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, SourceType, SourceId, DestinationType, InformationAssetIds, Purpose, EncryptedInTransit, EncryptedAtRest, EffectiveFrom, OwnerPersonId`.
  - `ReviseDataFlow` → ReviseDataFlowHandler; targets `DataFlow`; scope `ITechnologyInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, DataFlowId, ExpectedRevision, SourceType, SourceId, DestinationType, InformationAssetIds, Purpose, EncryptedInTransit, EncryptedAtRest, EffectiveFrom, OwnerPersonId, Lifecycle`.
- **Events emitted:** `DataFlowRevisionRecorded`
- **Additional replay facts:** `InformationAssetRevisionRecorded` [aggregate_fact], `TechnologyComponentRevisionRecorded` [aggregate_fact]
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“One tenant inventory identity has one record kind; flow endpoint/asset scope and encryption or approved-exception terms must match highest admitted carried classification, and effective date never moves backward.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Shared type exclusion must remain in one physical slot. Complete carried-asset relation is evidence for one atomic encryption rule; asset identity/lifecycle remains separate. No reason for all flows to share a revision.
  - Proof/authorization test: Handler resolves endpoints/assets and passes classification string/value; no live other aggregate enters flow. This is snapshot admission of another owner rule, not perpetual enforcement after asset reclassification.
  - Growth test: Current complete content incl asset ID list and highest observed classification, revision/kind flags. Asset list capped100; no stream lifetime bound because unlimited revisions append full lists.

## 29. DeclaredApplication

- **Identity:** Per-application identity. Initial declaration fields retained for changed-intent check. Revisions/retirement lack selected RequestId dedup. Relationships mix tuple-derived and caller ids and retry checks omit portions of intent.
- **Invariant:** An application has immutable creation intent, versioned metadata and retirement eligibility; retirement requires an approved successor for the exact source revision. Import reservation must prevent conflicting local changes until durable settlement.
- **Boundary:** split. Metadata/retirement and a narrow admission generation remain with application. Relationship identities have separate lifecycles. Splitting them requires source retirement/admission holds and unique active relationship tuple claims; no projected roster can enforce successor or active-source rule.
- **Current observations:** ROOT-01 (pending), ROOT-02 (pending), ROOT-04 (pending), ROOT-05 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: ResolveImportVisibility calls live ledger and mutates _created/_retired/_revision. Production ApplicationImportWriteGuard hydrates each source ledger and supplies committed event ids to constructor. Application state depends on external settlement proof at replay, not just own stream. RecordPendingImportEffect also calls ledger authorization.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Metadata/retirement and a narrow admission generation remain with application. Relationship identities have separate lifecycles. Splitting them requires source retirement/admission holds and unique active relationship tuple claims; no projected roster can enforce successor or active-source rule.
  - State/frame guard: Current metadata bounded but all relationship histories and all pending import effects retained; physical replay grows across every relationship and import interaction.

Owner-specific cross-boundary rule: ResolveImportVisibility calls live ledger and mutates _created/_retired/_revision. Production ApplicationImportWriteGuard hydrates each source ledger and supplies committed event ids to constructor. Application state depends on external settlement proof at replay, not just own stream. RecordPendingImportEffect also calls ledger authorization.

## 30. DeclaredSystemInstance

- **Identity:** Per instance minted/business id; declaration compares current fields, retirement rejects already-retired retry, revision commands have no selected logical request lookup.
- **Invariant:** One system instance has one immutable application parent and registered identity; metadata revisions and terminal retirement follow its own expected revision.
- **Boundary:** retain. Separating independent instances is already safe. Immutable parent binding and retirement gate belong with this instance, rather than parent roster state.
- **Current observations:** ROOT-05 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Parent identity is a value. Registration/retirement admission checks should pass value proof if parent status matters.
  - State/frame guard: Scalar current state is bounded; revisions still accumulate indefinitely in the physical stream. Repository hydration reads full history.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `DeclareSystemInstance` → DeclareSystemInstanceHandler; targets `DeclaredSystemInstance`; scope `IRequest<SystemInstanceRegistration>, IApplicationInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ApplicationInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Applications/ApplicationInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ApplicationId, ExpectedApplicationRevision, Name, Kind`.
  - `RetireSystemInstance` → RetireSystemInstanceHandler; targets `DeclaredSystemInstance`; scope `IApplicationInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ApplicationInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Applications/ApplicationInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ApplicationId, SystemInstanceId, ExpectedRevision, EffectiveAt, Reason`.
  - `ReviseSystemInstance` → ReviseSystemInstanceHandler; targets `DeclaredSystemInstance`; scope `IApplicationInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ApplicationInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Applications/ApplicationInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ApplicationId, SystemInstanceId, ExpectedRevision, Name, SourceIdentifier, AccessBoundary, Rationale`.
- **Events emitted:** `SystemInstanceRegistered`, `SystemInstanceRetired`, `SystemInstanceRevised`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“One system instance has one immutable application parent and registered identity; metadata revisions and terminal retirement follow its own expected revision.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Separating independent instances is already safe. Immutable parent binding and retirement gate belong with this instance, rather than parent roster state.
  - Proof/authorization test: Parent identity is a value. Registration/retirement admission checks should pass value proof if parent status matters.
  - Growth test: Scalar current state is bounded; revisions still accumulate indefinitely in the physical stream. Repository hydration reads full history.

## 31. EmailAddress

- **Identity:** UUIDv5 normalized address. Reserve owner retry safe; challenge issue handler mints random ID per invocation; recovery pair retry stable until overwritten. Expected-position OCC.
- **Invariant:** A normalized address reserves exactly one platform user; verification requires its current challenge; a claimed recovery binds one old/replacement pair.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Ownership and current-proof consumption require one authority; delivery outcomes do not. A recovery lifecycle can be separate only with durable claim/admission and revocation recovery.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-12 (pending), ACCESS-13 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: No live aggregate arguments; CompleteIdentityRecoveryHandler orchestrates address+two identity streams by separate writes.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Ownership and current-proof consumption require one authority; delivery outcomes do not. A recovery lifecycle can be separate only with durable claim/admission and revocation recovery.
  - State/frame guard: Current owner/verification, latest challenge+delivery and latest recovery+pair. Memory scalar, but all reissues/recoveries and delivery outcomes remain in lifetime address replay without bound.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `CompleteEmailChallenge` → CompleteEmailChallengeHandler; targets `EmailAddress`; scope `IEmailOwnershipRequest`; authorizer `[EmailOwnershipAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/UserIdentities/EmailOwnershipAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `UserId, EmailAddress, Token`.
  - `CompleteIdentityRecovery` → CompleteIdentityRecoveryHandler; targets `EmailAddress, UserIdentity`; scope `feature authorizer not explicit in interface`; authorizer `[IdentityRecoveryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/UserIdentities/IdentityRecoveryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `EmailAddress, ChallengeId, Token, OldIdentityId`.
  - `IssueEmailChallenge` → IssueEmailChallengeHandler; targets `EmailAddress`; scope `IEmailOwnershipRequest`; authorizer `[EmailOwnershipAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/UserIdentities/EmailOwnershipAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `UserId, EmailAddress`.
  - `ReserveEmail` → ReserveEmailHandler; targets `EmailAddress`; scope `IEmailOwnershipRequest`; authorizer `[EmailOwnershipAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/UserIdentities/EmailOwnershipAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `UserId, EmailAddress`.
  - `StartIdentityRecovery` → StartIdentityRecoveryHandler; targets `EmailAddress`; scope `feature authorizer not explicit in interface`; authorizer `no explicit IRequestAuthorizer source mapped; verify framework/default admission`; typed contract fields `EmailAddress`.
- **Events emitted:** `EmailAddressReserved`, `EmailAddressVerified`, `EmailChallengeDeliveryFailed`, `EmailChallengeDeliverySent`, `EmailChallengeIssued`, `IdentityRecoveryChallengeClaimed`, `IdentityRecoveryChallengeCompleted`, `IdentityRecoveryChallengeIssued`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“A normalized address reserves exactly one platform user; verification requires its current challenge; a claimed recovery binds one old/replacement pair.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Ownership and current-proof consumption require one authority; delivery outcomes do not. A recovery lifecycle can be separate only with durable claim/admission and revocation recovery.
  - Proof/authorization test: No live aggregate arguments; CompleteIdentityRecoveryHandler orchestrates address+two identity streams by separate writes.
  - Growth test: Current owner/verification, latest challenge+delivery and latest recovery+pair. Memory scalar, but all reissues/recoveries and delivery outcomes remain in lifetime address replay without bound.

## 32. EvidenceArtifact

- **Identity:** Tenant EvidenceStreams key artifactId; capture registration stable source identity; inspection is single transition; download uses requestId in event but never retains/compares it; OCC uses hydrated stream position. Portia appends against the hydrated physical stream position; a logical revision does not create another physical boundary.
- **Invariant:** Immutable content remains unavailable until clean inspection; malware alone can be released with attributed clean rescan; secret/invalid rejection is final.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Inspection and quarantine release share availability decision. Download authorization needs an admission proof against that decision but every historical download need not extend artifact lifecycle stream.
- **Current observations:** ASSURE-08 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Handlers establish permissions and captured source values; aggregate takes no live owner. Download URL is issued before event commit.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Inspection and quarantine release share availability decision. Download authorization needs an admission proof against that decision but every historical download need not extend artifact lifecycle stream.
  - State/frame guard: Registration/release and current status constant size, but every download appends one event forever and increments artifact revision; replay cost is unbounded by lifecycle length.

    - Helper/coordination: `EvidenceIntake`. Guards: Authorize before resolving exact retries; use the request authorization scope IRequest<EvidenceCapture>, IClientManagementMutationRequest. Validate tenant/program and typed owner identity against the target stream before loading or mutating it. ## 33. EvidenceHandlingPolicy

- **Identity:** UUIDv5 tenant+artifactId; expectedRevision; changed content binding conflicts; exact current assignment comparison wrongly includes generated AssignedAt. Portia appends against the hydrated physical stream position; a logical revision does not create another physical boundary.
- **Invariant:** One exact artifact identity has one current reviewed classification, changed only at expected revision with attributed rationale.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Current classification CAS must remain atomic; past classifications/history do not participate in that decision.
- **Current observations:** ASSURE-05 (pending), ASSURE-10 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Value source snapshot, immutable content identity; handler checks source/authority.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Current classification CAS must remain atomic; past classifications/history do not participate in that decision.
  - State/frame guard: Full History appended into view/event on every reclassification and capped at32; revision33 cannot classify artifact even if legitimate. Replay grows intrinsically with classification decisions.

  - Given the owner invariant (“One exact artifact identity has one current reviewed classification, changed only at expected revision with attributed rationale.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Current classification CAS must remain atomic; past classifications/history do not participate in that decision.
  - Proof/authorization test: Value source snapshot, immutable content identity; handler checks source/authority.
  - Growth test: Full History appended into view/event on every reclassification and capped at32; revision33 cannot classify artifact even if legitimate. Replay grows intrinsically with classification decisions.

  - Given the owner invariant (“One final personal management decision binds exact package/assertion/letter/outcome and acknowledges exactly every unresolved material item.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Known exceptions and acknowledgements form one accepted decision capsule; splitting them would permit signing a different set. Referenced source lifecycles stay independent.
  - Proof/authorization test: Source validator in handler admits exact values; no live aggregate argument. Commitments are declarations in sign-off, not mutable owned child lifecycles.
  - Growth test: One immutable content/event, lists are frozen decision content, not accumulating children;128KiB per-event ceiling must meet storage transport.
- **Existing test references:** [ExaminationSignOffTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/Examinations/ExaminationSignOffTests.cs), [RecordManagementExaminationReviewHandlerTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/Examinations/RecordManagementExaminationReviewHandlerTests.cs)

## 39. FindingAggregate

- **Identity:** tenant/findings/v5(programId,findingId); native requestId is selected during replay and exact intent compared for each business command. Same physical expected position and logical revision enforced. Routed creation can establish raise+initial action, valid rule15.
- **Invariant:** A finding closes only when at least one corrective action exists, every action is completed, evidence and rationales exist, and the closer is independent of finding/action owners and completers unless exactly waived; closed finding is immutable until reopened.
- **Boundary:** split. Separating corrective action lifecycles without coordinating membership/completion would allow action creation/completion races with closure. Keep a finding closure coordinator that atomically seals its action generation and validates authoritative completion/SoD proofs; separate action streams own work. Release or retry interrupted admissions with recovery. History already uses same-stream version-fenced fold, which is not a substitute projection check.
- **Current observations:** OPS-06 (pending), OPS-11 (pending), OPS-12 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Close receives live SeparationOfDutiesWaiver and invokes Allows. LinkAcceptance receives a same-stream immutable fold fenced by Version; acceptable proof of its own history, no live foreign aggregate. Other-owner acceptance status is handler admission value.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Separating corrective action lifecycles without coordinating membership/completion would allow action creation/completion races with closure. Keep a finding closure coordinator that atomically seals its action generation and validates authoritative completion/SoD proofs; separate action streams own work. Release or retry interrupted admissions with recovery. History already uses same-stream version-fenced fold, which is not a substitute projection check.
  - State/frame guard: {"retained": "Raised source/current triage, complete current action list including completed evidence/notes, current closure, one selected native receipt, last import cursor/pending marker. Prior closures/acceptances/history are in FindingHistoryFold.", "lifetimeReplay": "Action list capped at 50 for entire lifetime and replay refuses 51st. Reopen/revise/link/close history remains unbounded despite bounded selected receipt and read-side history; replay always traverses full stream."}

## 40. FindingMigrationAggregate

- **Identity:** tenant/finding-migrations/programId; migrationId chosen once; exact fence repeat accepted; only latest exact physical progress retry accepted; completion compares parity. Expected physical append revision protects cursor.
- **Invariant:** Technical process invariant: migration has one exact immutable source fence, advances one contiguous physical source offset after target import, and completes only with exact parity count/digest at the fence.
- **Boundary:** process. Cursor/fence/digest must be atomic for resumable import. This is a process manager, not a business aggregate; preserve its coordination and target-before-cursor order when reclassifying.
- **Current observations:** OPS-12 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Receives immutable FindingMigrationFence and DomainEventRecord value proofs; no foreign live aggregate. Handler commits target pair before cursor.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Cursor/fence/digest must be atomic for resumable import. This is a process manager, not a business aggregate; preserve its coordination and target-before-cursor order when reclassifying.
  - State/frame guard: {"retained": "One fence, next offset, imported count/digest, last source lineage entry and completion flag.", "lifetimeReplay": "In-memory state is constant size, but one progress event per source position means replay history grows with migrated source history; finite once sealed."}
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `MigrateFindingHistory` → MigrateFindingHistoryHandler; targets `FindingAggregate, FindingMigrationAggregate, RemediationMigrationFenceAggregate`; scope `system reaction principal`; authorizer `[MigrateFindingHistoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Remediation/MigrateFindingHistoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProgramId, MigrationId`.
- **Events emitted:** `FindingMigrationCompleted`, `FindingMigrationProgressed`, `FindingMigrationStarted`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“Technical process invariant: migration has one exact immutable source fence, advances one contiguous physical source offset after target import, and completes only with exact parity count/digest at the fence.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Cursor/fence/digest must be atomic for resumable import. This is a process manager, not a business aggregate; preserve its coordination and target-before-cursor order when reclassifying.
  - Proof/authorization test: Receives immutable FindingMigrationFence and DomainEventRecord value proofs; no foreign live aggregate. Handler commits target pair before cursor.
  - Growth test: {"retained": "One fence, next offset, imported count/digest, last source lineage entry and completion flag.", "lifetimeReplay": "In-memory state is constant size, but one progress event per source position means replay history grows with migrated source history; finite once sealed."}

## 41. FirmProfessionalDutyCatalog

- **Identity:** Global fixed CatalogId; requestID+intent+actor retry map; expected global Sequence and exact predecessor enforce conflicts/OCC.
- **Invariant:** One active signed designation per user+duty+tenant; refresh/revoke must supersede exact assertion with increasing authority revision and stable subject.
- **Boundary:** split. Per-duty predecessor lineage and tuple uniqueness matter; unrelated tenants/users do not require one global revision. Global assertion identity may need separate reservation.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-04 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Verified assertion and staff view passed as values after handler admission; no aggregate reads another.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Per-duty predecessor lineage and tuple uniqueness matter; unrelated tenants/users do not require one global revision. Global assertion identity may need separate reservation.
  - State/frame guard: Dictionaries of every designation, continuity starts, latest signed assertions; seenAssertionIDs and request responses grow every change. Domain replay grows all firm duty lifetimes.

- **Events emitted:** `FirmProfessionalDutyDesignationRecorded`, `FirmProfessionalDutyDesignationRevoked`

  - Given the owner invariant (“One active signed designation per user+duty+tenant; refresh/revoke must supersede exact assertion with increasing authority revision and stable subject.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.

  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.

## 43. ImmutableSnapshot

- **Identity:** Tenant / snapshots / context.RequestId minted creation identity; same canonical content/linkage/actor retries; one append. Portia appends against the hydrated physical stream position; a logical revision does not create another physical boundary.
- **Invariant:** One snapshot identity can freeze exactly one canonical manifest/hash with immutable, attributed amendment lineage.
- **Boundary:** retain. One immutable source capsule must be committed whole; amended snapshots already have separate identities and do not mutate parent.
- **Current observations:** ASSURE-13 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.

## 44. ImportBatch

- **Identity:** BatchIdFor derives tenant, length encoded source key/namespace and stable SubmissionId, distinguishing retry from new submission. Same digest retry no-ops; changed digest conflicts. Legacy Cancel accepts any canceled retry with expectedRevision<=Revision, ignoring changed reason/actor.
- **Invariant:** A stable source submission has exactly one immutable digest and bounded staged row set; the same submission identity cannot be rebound to changed rows.
- **Boundary:** retain. Splitting rows without an immutable digest/seal would lose complete submission identity. Keep a finite staged capsule; chunk storage if transport requires, without making each immutable row an aggregate.
- **Current observations:** ROOT-02 (pending), ROOT-05 (pending)

  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Source acceptance lives separately. ImportBatch should supply an immutable digest/row proof at admission, not be passed as a live aggregate into another stream.
  - State/frame guard: {"current": "Immutable row set and staging metadata.", "lifetimeReplay": "Intrinsically finite stage/cancel lifecycle; row cap200/48KiB are submission/transport limits rather than a lifetime fact cap. Their business/transport derivation still needs justification."}

## 45. IndependenceLedger

- **Identity:** Tenant singleton; global Sequence and per-request normalized intent+actor response maps. Exact retries generally recognized before sequence gates; all append OCC inherited.
- **Invariant:** Permanent tenant+person practice exclusivity and acceptance only against exact client service history, reviewed draft, personal acknowledgement and ratified-rule proofs.
- **Boundary:** split. Separating unchecked assignments or acceptance from source history would break real independence rules. Independent draft editing/history/evaluation lifecycles need no all-tenant atomicity once authoritative admission coordination exists.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-02 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.

## 48. InformationAsset

- **Identity:** Shared tenant+record address and foreign sentinels; RequestId-derived creation ID. Create compares mutable current content; revision exact retries stale. Expected physical append OCC.
- **Invariant:** One tenant inventory identity has one record kind and one revisioned information classification/retention/owner/lifecycle record.
- **Boundary:** retain. Shared type exclusion must survive; no rule requires all information assets together. Classification/retention values form one governed record, not its own collection lifecycle.
- **Current observations:** ROOTTECH-02 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Workforce owner validated by handler before admission; aggregate does not load other owners. Classification changes affect future flow admission observations, with no claimed perpetual cross-owner encryption invariant.
  - State/frame guard: Current content+revision+flags. No children; every revised classification/retention/lifecycle adds full lifetime fact. No natural cap and replay strategy not established by small state.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `RecordInformationAsset` → RecordInformationAssetHandler; targets `InformationAsset`; scope `IRequest<InformationAssetRegistration>, ITechnologyInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, Name, Classification, RetentionReference, OwnerPersonId`.
  - `ReviseInformationAsset` → ReviseInformationAssetHandler; targets `InformationAsset`; scope `ITechnologyInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, InformationAssetId, ExpectedRevision, Name, Classification, RetentionReference, OwnerPersonId, Lifecycle`.
- **Events emitted:** `InformationAssetRevisionRecorded`
- **Additional replay facts:** `DataFlowRevisionRecorded` [aggregate_fact], `TechnologyComponentRevisionRecorded` [aggregate_fact]
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“One tenant inventory identity has one record kind and one revisioned information classification/retention/owner/lifecycle record.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.

## 50. LocationRegister

- **Identity:** Tenant singleton address inventory-registers/locations; child locationId=RequestId. Record retries only at revision1, Revise expected childRevision; shared physical append OCC protects duplicate scan against concurrency.
- **Invariant:** Among active locations of the same tenant and immutable kind, normalized governed names are unique; each location changes at exact record revision.
- **Boundary:** split. Unchecked per-location split would lose name uniqueness on concurrent creates/rename/reactivation. A narrow name-claim owner can retain it while independent location lifecycle moves out of parent roster.
- **Current observations:** ROOTTECH-01 (pending), ROOTTECH-02 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Workforce owner admission done in handler; root receives values and reads no live aggregate.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Unchecked per-location split would lose name uniqueness on concurrent creates/rename/reactivation. A narrow name-claim owner can retain it while independent location lifecycle moves out of parent roster.
  - State/frame guard: Dictionary all locations including retired, current full content+revision. Replay accumulates every location/revision lifetime. No cap, whole inventory growth unbounded.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `RecordLocation` → RecordLocationHandler; targets `LocationRegister`; scope `IRequest<LocationRegistration>, ITechnologyInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, Kind, Name, OwnerPersonId`.
  - `ReviseLocation` → ReviseLocationHandler; targets `LocationRegister`; scope `ITechnologyInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, LocationId, ExpectedRevision, Name, OwnerPersonId, Lifecycle`.
- **Events emitted:** `LocationRevisionRecorded`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“Among active locations of the same tenant and immutable kind, normalized governed names are unique; each location changes at exact record revision.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.

## 51. ManagementAssertion

## 52. ManagementRepresentationLetter

## 53. Member

- **Identity:** UUIDv5 tenant+user. New episode minted on accepted re-registration. Current flags make some retries no-ops but no operation+episode identity fences old commands. Append OCC serializes member writes.
- **Invariant:** One tenant-user membership has one affiliation/expiry episode; deprovision closes authority and a fresh episode cannot start until authority cleanup completes.
- **Boundary:** retain. Affiliation, lifecycle and current episode must change together. Cleanup completion is an authority-release gate; membership roster/grants remain external.

## 54. MethodologyTemplate

- **Identity:** Platform bdgrz / methodology-templates / templateId; new version/review UUID minted within command, caller context.RequestId not passed. Create/revise/review/publish/retire retry checks include newly generated server timestamps. Pinned Portia uses full physical stream replay and expected physical position; no durable request dedup is supplied.
- **Invariant:** Publish current authored template only after independent review and distinct third-party approver; published content immutable; retirement stops further template revisions. Contributed records require exact accepted deidentified provenance.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Current draft/review/approval must share version identity; prior immutable published version bodies and all historical accepted-contribution records need not remain live parent state. A compact retirement/publication authority can coordinate independently owned version lifecycles.
- **Current observations:** ROOTASSURE-02 (pending), ROOTASSURE-04 (pending), CORE-01 (complete)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Accepts contribution ShareView value proof, not live owner; no direct reads. Validates applicability of exact digest/provenance while contributor review/acceptance is separately owned.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Current draft/review/approval must share version identity; prior immutable published version bodies and all historical accepted-contribution records need not remain live parent state. A compact retirement/publication authority can coordinate independently owned version lifecycles.
  - State/frame guard: Every version with entire record contents/review/approval and growing accepted contribution references retained and copied into every event; MaximumVersions64 prohibits further normal editing. Nominal256KiB bound is ineffective per CORE-01.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `ApproveMethodologyTemplateVersion` → ApproveMethodologyTemplateVersionHandler; targets `MethodologyTemplate`; scope `IPlatformOperatorRequest`; authorizer `[PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TemplateId, Version, ApprovalRationale`.
  - `CreateMethodologyTemplate` → CreateMethodologyTemplateHandler; targets `MethodologyTemplate`; scope `IPlatformOperatorRequest`; authorizer `[PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TemplateId, Content, ChangeNotes`.
  - `RetireMethodologyTemplate` → RetireMethodologyTemplateHandler; targets `MethodologyTemplate`; scope `IPlatformOperatorRequest`; authorizer `[PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TemplateId, ExpectedRevision, EffectiveOn, Rationale`.
  - `ReviewMethodologyTemplate` → ReviewMethodologyTemplateHandler; targets `MethodologyTemplate`; scope `IPlatformOperatorRequest`; authorizer `[PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TemplateId, Version, Outcome, Comments`.
  - `ReviseMethodologyTemplateDraft` → ReviseMethodologyTemplateDraftHandler; targets `MethodologyTemplate`; scope `IPlatformOperatorRequest`; authorizer `[PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TemplateId, ExpectedRevision, Content, ChangeNotes`.
- **Events emitted:** `MethodologyTemplateCreated`, `MethodologyTemplateDraftReviewed`, `MethodologyTemplateDraftRevised`, `MethodologyTemplateRetired`, `MethodologyTemplateVersionPublished`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“Publish current authored template only after independent review and distinct third-party approver; published content immutable; retirement stops further template revisions. Contributed records require exact accepted deidentified provenance.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Current draft/review/approval must share version identity; prior immutable published version bodies and all historical accepted-contribution records need not remain live parent state. A compact retirement/publication authority can coordinate independently owned version lifecycles.
  - Proof/authorization test: Accepts contribution ShareView value proof, not live owner; no direct reads. Validates applicability of exact digest/provenance while contributor review/acceptance is separately owned.
  - Growth test: Every version with entire record contents/review/approval and growing accepted contribution references retained and copied into every event; MaximumVersions64 prohibits further normal editing. Nominal256KiB bound is ineffective per CORE-01.
- **Existing test references:** [MethodologyTemplateCommandHandlerTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/MethodologyTemplates/MethodologyTemplateCommandHandlerTests.cs), [MethodologyTemplateContributionTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/MethodologyTemplates/MethodologyTemplateContributionTests.cs), [MethodologyTemplateTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/MethodologyTemplates/MethodologyTemplateTests.cs)

## 55. MethodologyTemplateContribution

- **Identity:** Tenant / methodology-template-contributions / contributionId; one immutable proposal; current review/acceptance immutable. Exact retries compare server generated ProposedAt/ReviewedAt/AcceptedAt and proposal only replays at revision1. Pinned Portia uses full physical stream replay and expected physical position; no durable request dedup is supplied.
- **Invariant:** Only immutable proposed deidentified record that receives independent approved review then acceptance by a third platform operator becomes shareable; tenant/source lineage stays in tenant-owned stream.
- **Boundary:** retain. These three irreversible decisions jointly authorize sharing one content capsule; separating author/review/acceptance without exact value/version gate would leak unapproved content. No retained child roster.
- **Current observations:** ROOTASSURE-04 (pending), CORE-01 (complete)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: No live aggregate parameters or reads; ShareableContent produces deidentified value capsule. Review remains human accepted judgment; UUID literal check alone is not claimed complete semantic deidentification.
  - State/frame guard: Exactly one proposed record, one review, one acceptance; lifetime at most3 facts. Fixed payload references max50, not an unbounded child history. Nominal128KiB guard measures base DomainEvent (CORE-01).
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `AcceptTemplateContribution` → AcceptTemplateContributionHandler; targets `MethodologyTemplateContribution`; scope `IPlatformOperatorRequest`; authorizer `[PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ContributionId, AcceptanceRationale`.
  - `ProposeDeidentifiedTemplateContribution` → ProposeDeidentifiedTemplateContributionHandler; targets `MethodologyTemplateContribution`; scope `IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ContributionId, SourceRecordId, SourceVersion, ProposedContent, DeidentificationRationale`.
  - `ReviewTemplateContribution` → ReviewTemplateContributionHandler; targets `MethodologyTemplateContribution`; scope `IPlatformOperatorRequest`; authorizer `[PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ContributionId, Outcome, ReviewNotes`.
- **Events emitted:** `MethodologyTemplateContributionAccepted`, `MethodologyTemplateContributionProposed`, `MethodologyTemplateContributionReviewed`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“Only immutable proposed deidentified record that receives independent approved review then acceptance by a third platform operator becomes shareable; tenant/source lineage stays in tenant-owned stream.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: These three irreversible decisions jointly authorize sharing one content capsule; separating author/review/acceptance without exact value/version gate would leak unapproved content. No retained child roster.
  - Proof/authorization test: No live aggregate parameters or reads; ShareableContent produces deidentified value capsule. Review remains human accepted judgment; UUID literal check alone is not claimed complete semantic deidentification.
  - Growth test: Exactly one proposed record, one review, one acceptance; lifetime at most3 facts. Fixed payload references max50, not an unbounded child history. Nominal128KiB guard measures base DomainEvent (CORE-01).
- **Existing test references:** [MethodologyTemplateCommandHandlerTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/MethodologyTemplates/MethodologyTemplateCommandHandlerTests.cs), [MethodologyTemplateContributionTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/MethodologyTemplates/MethodologyTemplateContributionTests.cs)

## 56. OperationalProcessRegister

- **Identity:** Tenant singleton inventory-registers/operational-processes; processId=RequestId. Record exact retry only current revision1; Revise expected child revision; physical append OCC on whole register.
- **Invariant:** Within tenant, active normalized process names unique and each governed process content/lifecycle changes at its exact revision.
- **Boundary:** split. Processes have independent identities/lifecycles. Separate record owners require authoritative name reservation, not projected duplicate lookup.
- **Current observations:** ROOTTECH-01 (pending), ROOTTECH-02 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Owner existence admission outside root; all arguments values.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Processes have independent identities/lifecycles. Separate record owners require authoritative name reservation, not projected duplicate lookup.
  - State/frame guard: All process rows including retired held forever; every process revision in same lifetime stream. Current roster and replay grow across independent processes.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `RecordOperationalProcess` → RecordOperationalProcessHandler; targets `OperationalProcessRegister`; scope `IRequest<OperationalProcessRegistration>, ITechnologyInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, Name, Purpose, OwnerPersonId`.
  - `ReviseOperationalProcess` → ReviseOperationalProcessHandler; targets `OperationalProcessRegister`; scope `ITechnologyInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, OperationalProcessId, ExpectedRevision, Name, Purpose, OwnerPersonId, Lifecycle`.
- **Events emitted:** `OperationalProcessRevisionRecorded`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“Within tenant, active normalized process names unique and each governed process content/lifecycle changes at its exact revision.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Processes have independent identities/lifecycles. Separate record owners require authoritative name reservation, not projected duplicate lookup.
  - Proof/authorization test: Owner existence admission outside root; all arguments values.
  - Growth test: All process rows including retired held forever; every process revision in same lifetime stream. Current roster and replay grow across independent processes.
- **Existing test references:** [InventoryRegisterAggregateTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/TechnologyInventory/InventoryRegisterAggregateTests.cs), [InventoryRegisterReadConsistencyTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/TechnologyInventory/InventoryRegisterReadConsistencyTests.cs)

## 57. Person

- **Identity:** Create uses context.RequestId as personId; initial normalized intent retry safe. Revisions/correlation use expectedRevision but exact successful revision retry becomes stale.
- **Invariant:** One recorded workforce person has immutable creation identity, revisioned contact content and at most one current platform membership correlation.
- **Boundary:** retain. Fields/correlation at a revision must be coherent; another person and roster do not need same stream. This is primarily governed record consistency rather than a cross-member invariant.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-10 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Record handler needs no owner lookup. Correlation admission validates external member, never changes platform authority.
  - State/frame guard: Scalar initial/current contact, correlation and revision. Unlimited revisions add lifetime history; no natural cap or workload/replay bound proved.
- **Commands and handlers:**

  - `RevisePerson` → RevisePersonHandler; targets `Person`; scope `IWorkforceRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [WorkforceAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Workforce/WorkforceAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, PersonId, ExpectedRevision, DisplayName`.
- **Events emitted:** `PersonMembershipCorrelated`, `PersonRecorded`, `PersonRevised`

  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.

  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.

## 58. PlatformOperatorRoster

- **Identity:** Fixed platform roster ID; expected physical position serializes last-operator decisions. Seed idempotent; grant/revoke uses current set only and does not identify old operation.
- **Invariant:** There must always be at least one active platform operator, and only current operators can grant/revoke.
- **Boundary:** retain. Splitting active operator grants without authoritative count/hold can allow simultaneous last removals or revoked actor grants. Current collection is justified by this rule.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-08 (in_progress)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: No cross-owner reads; actor operator status owned inside same stream.
  - State/frame guard: Active operator HashSet; no parent tenant/member roster. Grant/revoke history grows indefinitely despite small current set; explicit replay strategy/cost evidence absent.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `GrantPlatformOperator` → GrantPlatformOperatorHandler; targets `PlatformOperatorRoster`; scope `IPlatformOperatorRequest`; authorizer `[PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `UserId, Reason, CommandId`.
  - `RevokePlatformOperator` → RevokePlatformOperatorHandler; targets `PlatformOperatorRoster`; scope `IPlatformOperatorRequest`; authorizer `[PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/PlatformOperatorAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `UserId, Reason, CommandId, AssignmentId`.
  - `SeedPlatformOperatorRoster` → SeedPlatformOperatorRosterHandler; targets `PlatformOperatorRoster`; scope `feature authorizer not explicit in interface`; authorizer `[SeedPlatformOperatorRosterAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/SeedPlatformOperatorRosterAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `RosterId`.
- **Events emitted:** `PlatformOperatorGranted`, `PlatformOperatorRevoked`, `PlatformOperatorRosterSeeded`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“There must always be at least one active platform operator, and only current operators can grant/revoke.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Splitting active operator grants without authoritative count/hold can allow simultaneous last removals or revoked actor grants. Current collection is justified by this rule.
  - Proof/authorization test: No cross-owner reads; actor operator status owned inside same stream.
  - Growth test: Active operator HashSet; no parent tenant/member roster. Grant/revoke history grows indefinitely despite small current set; explicit replay strategy/cost evidence absent.
- **Existing test references:** [OperatorPortfolioE2ETests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/E2E/OperatorPortfolioE2ETests.cs), [PlatformOperatorRosterTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/Tenants/PlatformOperatorRosterTests.cs)

## 59. Policy

- **Identity:** tenant/policies/policyId derived from tenant+program+normalized identifier; Create binds stable createRequestId+initial content. Mutations use logical Revision/currentVersion; Review and periodic review do not dedup decision identities, so same retry can append again.
- **Invariant:** A policy has one pending revision/retirement; immutable approved version intervals form one successor line, activation needs the latest accepted independent review and independent approval, and retired/approved content cannot be edited.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Draft authors, accepted review and approval must stay atomic to avoid approving changed or self-reviewed terms. Old immutable versions and full decisions need not be active command state; preserve current predecessor plus exact decision target, and project history.
- **Current observations:** OPS-11 (pending), OPS-13 (pending), OPS-15 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Policy checks live waiver Allows in CheckIndependence; handler supplies permissions/other-owner impact digest values at admission.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Draft authors, accepted review and approval must stay atomic to avoid approving changed or self-reviewed terms. Old immutable versions and full decisions need not be active command state; preserve current predecessor plus exact decision target, and project history.
  - State/frame guard: {"retained": "Current pending draft/author set/review, all full approved versions, every decision, current retirement and cadence data.", "lifetimeReplay": "All version bodies/decision history retained; 48KiB/20k body limits control each event/version, not lifetime growth or replay."}
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `ApprovePolicy` → ApprovePolicyHandler; targets `Policy`; scope `IRequest<PolicyVersionView>, IProgramScopedRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProgramId, PolicyId, ExpectedRevision, AcceptedReviewDecisionId, EffectiveFrom, Major, Rationale`.

  - `ConfirmPolicyReview` → ConfirmPolicyReviewHandler; targets `Policy`; scope `IRequest<PolicyDecisionView>, IProgramScopedRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProgramId, PolicyId, ExpectedVersion, Rationale`.

## 60. PolicyDistributionCampaign

- **Identity:** tenant/policy-distribution-campaigns/context.RequestId; per-person acknowledgement/completion/waiver IDs. No logical expectedRevision on most commands, physical OCC only. Launch compares a subset of terms and result replay only compares result ID.
- **Invariant:** One campaign freezes exact material and launch audience; results must name that version/hash/text and a currently included person, and closure freezes totals and rejects later results.
- **Boundary:** split. Acknowledgement/completion/exception are independent per-person lifecycles. Split them by campaign+person; retain a narrow campaign terms/closure owner and use a seal/hold protocol before accepting final totals so no result is admitted after campaign close. Audience amendments/rosters/history belong to projections and admission records.
- **Current observations:** OPS-07 (pending), OPS-16 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Receives immutable material/roster values; no live aggregate. Handler reads policy/roster at admission and guards reconciliation against latest roster identity.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Acknowledgement/completion/exception are independent per-person lifecycles. Split them by campaign+person; retain a narrow campaign terms/closure owner and use a seal/hold protocol before accepting final totals so no result is admitted after campaign close. Audience amendments/rosters/history belong to projections and admission records.
  - State/frame guard: {"retained": "Every participant ever seen, current result/exception, launch order, every audience amendment and every reconciled snapshot identity.", "lifetimeReplay": "MaximumAudience10,000 limits launch only; reconciliation can add unlimited new people, expired exceptions and snapshots accumulate. Full audience batches are retained and replayed."}
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `AcknowledgePolicy` → AcknowledgePolicyHandler; targets `PolicyDistributionCampaign`; scope `IRequest<CampaignAcknowledgementView>, IProgramReadRequest`; authorizer `[ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProgramId, CampaignId, PersonId, PolicyVersion, ContentSha256, AcknowledgementText`.

## 61. PopulationSnapshot

- **Identity:** Tenant / population-snapshots / context.RequestId; retry compares exact content/lineage/actor; chunk keys deterministic; physical expected position. Portia appends against the hydrated physical stream position; a logical revision does not create another physical boundary.
- **Invariant:** Freeze exactly one canonical immutable population identity, optionally a manifest over ordered immutable chunk identities; amendments have separate roots.
- **Boundary:** retain. Rows may live outside root because immutable hashes/manifests bind exact order/count; root commit makes snapshot visible only after chunks exist. Current implementation has retry-based recovery, not a recovery reactor.
- **Current observations:** ASSURE-13 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Freezer reads predecessor before passing root identity; aggregate accepts values. Chunk contents verified by PopulationSnapshotContent outside root.
  - State/frame guard: One fact per snapshot, up to500 inline rows otherwise compact manifest; lifetime replay constant. Parent lineage policy nevertheless rejects beyond8 links.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `AcceptAccessPopulation` → AcceptAccessPopulationHandler; targets `AccessPopulation, PopulationSnapshot, PopulationSnapshotChunk`; scope `IRequest<AccessPopulationAcceptance>, IAccessReviewMutationRequest`; authorizer `[AccessReviewAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessReviews/AccessReviewAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, PopulationId, ExpectedRevision, Attestation`.
    - Helper/coordination: `PopulationSnapshotFreezer`. Guards: Authorize before resolving exact retries; use the request authorization scope IRequest<AccessPopulationAcceptance>, IAccessReviewMutationRequest. Validate tenant/program and typed owner identity against the target stream before loading or mutating it. For an accepted operation identity, compare owner, lifecycle episode, original actor, canonical authored intent and original expected revision; exact retry returns the original result without appending. For a new operation, enforce expected revision/physical append OCC. If a repeated mutation has no stable typed identity or revision, add it before enabling retries. For every other owner, pass a server-created immutable proof with source identity, event/position, revision, digest and required facts; do not pass a live aggregate as authority. Owner-specific cross-boundary rule: Freezer reads predecessor before passing root identity; aggregate accepts values. Chunk contents verified by PopulationSnapshotContent outside root.

## 62. PopulationSnapshotChunk

- **Identity:** UUIDv5 namespace + tenant/snapshot/index; Store compares kind/digest; one append; one technical record per chunk. Portia appends against the hydrated physical stream position; a logical revision does not create another physical boundary.
- **Invariant:** Technical invariant only: one snapshot/index stores one immutable kind+row digest, changed bytes conflict. No independent business decision or lifecycle.
- **Boundary:** process. Chunking is storage for the snapshot-owned freeze invariant; chunks can be moved to content-addressed storage without losing a business decision if manifest visibility and integrity remain atomic.
- **Current observations:** ASSURE-14 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: No live aggregate read; freezer writes ordered chunks then manifest; interrupted operation relies on caller retry and leaves invisible chunks.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Chunking is storage for the snapshot-owned freeze invariant; chunks can be moved to content-addressed storage without losing a business decision if manifest visibility and integrity remain atomic.
  - State/frame guard: One bounded chunk, one fact per stream; count increases with snapshot size, not lifetime writes.

    - Helper/coordination: `PopulationSnapshotFreezer`.
