## 63. ProgramRollover

- **Identity:** Tenant / program-rollovers / rolloverId; proposal content compare; approval per version no-op; revision command checks before comparison; exact successor receipt compares timestamp. Portia appends against the hydrated physical stream position; a logical revision does not create another physical boundary.
- **Invariant:** One exact carry-forward proposal version needs independent approved decision before recording exact successor period/plan identities; completed source provenance remains immutable.
- **Boundary:** process. Proposal/current approval and execution claim must coordinate to prevent approved version changing while successor is created. Prior proposal history is unnecessary state; successor creation acknowledgement belongs to recoverable process storage.
- **Current observations:** ASSURE-15 (pending), ASSURE-17 (pending), ASSURE-11 (pending), CORE-01 (complete)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Receives sourcePeriod/outcome VIEW values rather than live aggregates; checks immutable closed-source provenance during admission.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Proposal/current approval and execution claim must coordinate to prevent approved version changing while successor is created. Prior proposal history is unnecessary state; successor creation acknowledgement belongs to recoverable process storage.
  - State/frame guard: All versions/approvals copied into each event, max32 versions,256KiB ceiling. Records/commitments are frozen plan choices, not copied source lifecycles.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `ApproveProgramRollover` → ApproveProgramRolloverHandler; targets `ProgramRollover`; scope `IProgramScopedRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProgramId, RolloverId, Version, Decision, Rationale`.
  - `ProposeProgramRollover` → ProposeProgramRolloverHandler; targets `ProgramRollover`; scope `IProgramScopedRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProgramId, RolloverId, Content`.
- **Events emitted:** `ProgramNextPeriodCreated`, `ProgramRolloverApproved`, `ProgramRolloverDecisionRecorded`, `ProgramRolloverProposed`, `ProgramRolloverRevised`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“One exact carry-forward proposal version needs independent approved decision before recording exact successor period/plan identities; completed source provenance remains immutable.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Proposal/current approval and execution claim must coordinate to prevent approved version changing while successor is created. Prior proposal history is unnecessary state; successor creation acknowledgement belongs to recoverable process storage.
  - Proof/authorization test: Receives sourcePeriod/outcome VIEW values rather than live aggregates; checks immutable closed-source provenance during admission.
  - Growth test: All versions/approvals copied into each event, max32 versions,256KiB ceiling. Records/commitments are frozen plan choices, not copied source lifecycles.

## 64. ProviderAssuranceRegister

- **Identity:** Tenant singleton provider-assurance/tenant. report/review/gap create IDs=RequestId. Full decision map normalizes authored intent and returns original result before current external sources; child revisions and physical append OCC. No general retry defect asserted for report/review/gap methods.
- **Invariant:** Each report preserves provider identity/revision; personal review binds exact same-provider evidence kind/current report revision and valid period/currency/cadence; each gap closes once only on newer/different source, and accepted-risk adoption cannot close gap or occur after closure.
- **Boundary:** split. Reports, personal reviews and gaps have own identities/lifecycles. Review-current-report admission is real coordination, as is open-gap gate for risk adoption; preserve with exact immutable proof/hold rather than global all-tenant roster. Other providers need no atomic revision.
- **Current observations:** ROOTTECH-03 (pending), ROOTTECH-04 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Handler supplies observed provider materiality/revision and verified artifact/closure/risk values. Aggregate accesses retained reports to ensure exact review evidence because same root owns them; it never reads a live external aggregate. Split must preserve this atomic report-review relationship with proof/hold.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Reports, personal reviews and gaps have own identities/lifecycles. Review-current-report admission is real coordination, as is open-gap gate for risk adoption; preserve with exact immutable proof/hold rather than global all-tenant roster. Other providers need no atomic revision.
  - State/frame guard: All current reports with embedded extracted facts, all immutable reviews, all open/closed gaps, each gap lifetime risk adoption array, every request normalized intent bytes/response. Caps200 each record-kind/provider and50 links/gap do not bound total tenant/revision replay; expired adoption history never pruned. All typed JsonContext frame guards correct.

## 66. ReadinessLedger

## 70. RiskEvaluation

- **Identity:** tenant/risk-evaluations/riskId; assessment/acceptance IDs are stable request identities; shared evaluation Revision. Reused assessment compares only phase/score/method, acceptance only residual+approver, missing full changed intent comparison.

Keep a narrow risk assessment/acceptance decision context; independent historical assessment/acceptance decisions can have their own immutable IDs with current decision owner binding exact proofs.

  - Boundary/coordination guard: Separating current treatment/residual from acceptance without a coordinating owner can accept stale terms. Keep a narrow risk assessment/acceptance decision context; independent historical assessment/acceptance decisions can have their own immutable IDs with current decision owner binding exact proofs.

## 71. RiskGovernanceLedger

- **Identity:** tenant/risk-governance/programId; child riskId/treatmentId/actionId, per-risk logical revision, physical program OCC. TriggerId derived from risk/kind/source. Action edit has complete stable intent tuple; submit replay only sees submissionId and several reviews lack replay handling.
- **Invariant:** Within each risk/control tuple, one pending treatment assertion and one accepted version per control; independent review controls acceptance. Within one treatment action, only unfinished work can edit/cancel and completion needs current fulfilled evidence plus independent review.
- **Boundary:** split. No rule spans different risks. Split per-risk current governance first; then per-control treatment assertion and per-action lifecycle. Preserve per-control supersession by tuple-derived stream, keep action accountable/submitter+review context atomic, and pass risk treatment/fulfilled evidence admission proofs. Trigger inbox/status is a projection/process concern.
- **Current observations:** OPS-04 (pending), OPS-11 (pending), OPS-15 (pending), OPS-16 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Receives risk treatment kind and fulfilled evidence ID set as admission values, appropriate under rule13 if not claimed continuously current. Reviews accept live waiver and call Allows.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: No rule spans different risks. Split per-risk current governance first; then per-control treatment assertion and per-action lifecycle. Preserve per-control supersession by tuple-derived stream, keep action accountable/submitter+review context atomic, and pass risk treatment/fulfilled evidence admission proofs. Trigger inbox/status is a projection/process concern.
  - State/frame guard: {"retained": "Every program risk with owner, all treatment assertions, all triggers, every action/completion history, proposer/submitter/cancellation/edit request maps.", "lifetimeReplay": "Risks/actions/triggers/edit and completion retries grow command state and replay indefinitely."}
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `AddRiskTreatmentAction` → AddRiskTreatmentActionHandler; targets `RiskGovernanceLedger`; scope `IRequest<RiskTreatmentActionRegistration>, IProgramScopedRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProgramId, RiskId, ExpectedRevision, Title, TargetState, ExpectedEvidence, DueOn, AccountableMemberId`.

## 72. RiskMethod

- **Identity:** tenant/risk-methods/v5(tenant,risk-method:program); versionId derives current sequential number. Publish lacks stable request identity; exact retry with original expectedVersion conflicts instead of no-op.
- **Invariant:** The program method advances sequentially by expected published version and each immutable definition has complete valid 5x5 scales and permitted appetite.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Scales/appetite together define one method version and should remain atomic; historical versions can be immutable definition records read as values while narrow owner retains current version identity. No need split scale members.
- **Current observations:** OPS-13 (pending), OPS-15 (pending)

  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.

  - Boundary/coordination guard: Scales/appetite together define one method version and should remain atomic; historical versions can be immutable definition records read as values while narrow owner retains current version identity. No need split scale members.
  - State/frame guard: {"retained": "All method versions with full scales, appetite and author/time.", "lifetimeReplay": "State/replay grows with every method publication; fixed scale size bounds one version only."}
- **Commands and handlers:**

- **Events emitted:** `RiskMethodVersionPublished`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.

  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.

  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.

## 73. Role

- **Identity:** Per tenant roleId, built-in IDs stable; system-only Define gate. Define ignores changed creation content; Rename is idempotent by normalized current name; expected-position OCC.
- **Invariant:** One role has one active/deleted lifecycle, normalized display name and built-in protection.
- **Boundary:** retain. Display name and deletion are one role lifecycle; permissions/teams correctly separate identity/lifecycles and projections.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-09 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.

## 75. SeparationOfDutiesWaiver

- **Identity:** Create waiverId=context.RequestId. Record normalized terms compare; approval exact approver retry no-op and changed approver conflict. Expected-position OCC.
- **Invariant:** Exact record/version/action/beneficiary exception can be approved once only by a different administrator who is neither requester nor beneficiary, during its validity window.
- **Boundary:** retain. Request scope/actors and approval must remain one authority. Another waiver has independent lifecycle.
- **Current observations:** none
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Member eligibility is handler admission through membership reader. Aggregate receives values; it owns separation among its own recorded actors, not external role truth.
  - State/frame guard: One request, one approval; max two facts naturally by lifecycle, not invented cap.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `ApproveSeparationOfDutiesWaiver` → ApproveSeparationOfDutiesWaiverHandler; targets `SeparationOfDutiesWaiver`; scope `IRequest<SeparationOfDutiesWaiverView>, ISeparationOfDutiesWaiverAdminRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [SeparationOfDutiesWaiverAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/SeparationOfDutiesWaiverAuthorizer.cs), [EvidenceRedactionWaiverScopeAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Evidence/EvidenceRedactionWaiverScopeAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, WaiverId`.
  - `RecordSeparationOfDutiesWaiver` → RecordSeparationOfDutiesWaiverHandler; targets `SeparationOfDutiesWaiver`; scope `IRequest<SeparationOfDutiesWaiverView>, ISeparationOfDutiesWaiverAdminRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [SeparationOfDutiesWaiverAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/SeparationOfDutiesWaiverAuthorizer.cs), [EvidenceRedactionWaiverScopeAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Evidence/EvidenceRedactionWaiverScopeAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, Scope, BeneficiaryUserId, Rationale, ExpiresAt`.
- **Events emitted:** `SeparationOfDutiesWaiverApproved`, `SeparationOfDutiesWaiverRecorded`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“Exact record/version/action/beneficiary exception can be approved once only by a different administrator who is neither requester nor beneficiary, during its validity window.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Request scope/actors and approval must remain one authority. Another waiver has independent lifecycle.
  - Proof/authorization test: Member eligibility is handler admission through membership reader. Aggregate receives values; it owns separation among its own recorded actors, not external role truth.
  - Growth test: One request, one approval; max two facts naturally by lifecycle, not invented cap.

## 76. ServiceIdentity

- **Identity:** Create context.RequestId; normalized initial intent compare. Revision mutation uses expectedRevision; exact revision retry stale. Date-dependent validation runs before create-retry recognition.
- **Invariant:** One governed nonhuman identity has exactly one accountable person/team owner, approved purpose, valid lifecycle/expiry and review date within one year.
- **Boundary:** retain. Owner/purpose/review/active expiry must be coherent at each record revision; other identities and roster need no shared stream.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-10 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.

- **Events emitted:** `ServiceIdentityRecorded`, `ServiceIdentityRevised`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“One governed nonhuman identity has exactly one accountable person/team owner, approved purpose, valid lifecycle/expiry and review date within one year.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.

  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.

## 77. SignificantChange

- **Identity:** Per change id is a valid stable creation identity. SameInitial/revision/assessment helpers compare server RecordedAt; fresh handler clock makes an exact client retry conflict or append. No selected RequestId guard.
- **Invariant:** One change version may be assessed once; current-version independent description decision and required follow-up eligibility gate advancement.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Keep current version, assessment and eligibility/independence gate atomic. Historical versions/decisions are immutable histories; do not split current gate simply by entity nouns. Required work selection can be sealed exact-version content with authority proofs.
- **Current observations:** ROOT-06 (pending), ROOT-07 (pending), CORE-01 (complete)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.

  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Linked review work/description admission should carry exact scope/version proof. Receiver owns only its own current version gate.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Keep current version, assessment and eligibility/independence gate atomic. Historical versions/decisions are immutable histories; do not split current gate simply by entity nouns. Required work selection can be sealed exact-version content with authority proofs.
  - State/frame guard: Full version/assessment/decision chronology is embedded in every event and _current; lifetime32 versions is an invented growth limit. Physical replay and cumulative serialization grow even when current presentation is small.

## 78. SystemBoundary

- **Identity:** Tenant+boundaryId, create handler derives from RequestId, draft1=UUIDv5 boundary+draft1, successor draftID supplied from stable request. Create compares initial content even after revisions. Review DecisionId=RequestId but no duplicate/change check; revision/approval retries fail after success. Expected physical append OCC.
- **Invariant:** A boundary has one current draft; approval requires latest accepted review of exact draft revision, an independent/waived actor and strictly increasing approved effective date; exact-scope responsibility changes cannot create retroactive unwaived decision conflict.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Retain the complete conflict-relevant responsibility/decision state for the current exact boundary draft scope in the same authoritative decision boundary as its review and approval. Do not turn current assignments into independent unconstrained aggregate streams. Narrow accumulated past-scope/version histories and external waiver behavior; a physical draft/publication split is a conditional future design, not the present audit verdict. The collection protects an actual atomic rule over its members: one member cannot hold overlapping work and review/approval responsibilities for the same exact version/revision without an accepted exact waiver, and a later backdated assignment cannot invalidate a prior unwaived review/approval decision. This falls under rule4. Record identity alone does not justify separating assignments.
- **Current observations:** ROOTACCESS-01 (pending), ROOTACCESS-02 (pending), ROOTACCESS-04 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: No live reader inside class, but methods receive actual SeparationOfDutiesWaiver aggregates and call their Allows/tenant behavior; violates value-proof rule. Program/reference existence and preview completeness are checked by handler admission.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Retain the complete conflict-relevant responsibility/decision state for the current exact boundary draft scope in the same authoritative decision boundary as its review and approval. Do not turn current assignments into independent unconstrained aggregate streams. Narrow accumulated past-scope/version histories and external waiver behavior; a physical draft/publication split is a conditional future design, not the present audit verdict. The collection protects an actual atomic rule over its members: one member cannot hold overlapping work and review/approval responsibilities for the same exact version/revision without an accepted exact waiver, and a later backdated assignment cannot invalidate a prior unwaived review/approval decision. This falls under rule4. Record identity alone does not justify separating assignments.
  - State/frame guard: Initial content, current complete draft and author/review flags, latest approved ID/effective date, approvedVersions map and every scope responsibility set/decision list. No invented lifetime cap. Both memory and replay grow unbounded across revisions, successor approvals, assignments and duplicate reviews.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `ApproveBoundary` → ApproveBoundaryHandler; targets `SystemBoundary`; scope `IBoundaryAuthoringRequest, IBoundaryProgramResourceRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, BoundaryId, DraftVersionId, ExpectedRevision, AcceptedReviewDecisionId, EffectiveFrom, Rationale, ImpactDigest`.
  - `AssignResponsibility` → AssignResponsibilityHandler; targets `CommitmentDraft, ControlDraft, SystemBoundary`; scope `IRbacManagementRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [RbacManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/RbacManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, MemberUserId, Type, RecordType, RecordId, VersionId, ScopeRevision, EffectiveFrom, SeparationOfDutiesWaiverIds, Scope`.
  - `CreateBoundary` → CreateBoundaryHandler; targets `SystemBoundary`; scope `IRequest<BoundaryRegistration>, IBoundaryAuthoringRequest, IProgramScopedRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProgramId, Content`.
  - `DiscardBoundaryDraft` → DiscardBoundaryDraftHandler; targets `SystemBoundary`; scope `IBoundaryAuthoringRequest, IBoundaryProgramResourceRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, BoundaryId, DraftVersionId, ExpectedRevision, Rationale`.

## 79. SystemDescription

- **Identity:** Tenant+descriptionId, explicit program fenced at replay. VersionId uses RequestId in handlers; scope/lineage/content hash immutable. Review key=description+version+reviewer. ExportId=RequestId. Exact create/approve/revise retry semantics incomplete. Expected physical append OCC.
- **Invariant:** Exact immutable narrative version may be approved only after independent review, all required sections complete/justified, exact current sources, all TypeII change dispositions and frozen close snapshot; approved content/snapshot binding cannot be changed.
- **Boundary:** split. Sections/source/change manifests are complete approval evidence and need authoritative completeness, not arbitrary per-item projections. Review/disposition must coordinate with exact version approval. Independent export generation and binding lifecycle do not need narrative revision; all historical versions need not load to edit current draft.
- **Current observations:** ROOTACCESS-03 (pending), ROOTACCESS-04 (pending), ROOTACCESS-05 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Aggregate never receives live external aggregate. Create handler hydrates examination/baseline then passes source/scope values; BindSources handlers reconcile source values at admission. These are appropriate owner proofs, assuming caller trust route intact.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Sections/source/change manifests are complete approval evidence and need authoritative completeness, not arbitrary per-item projections. Review/disposition must coordinate with exact version approval. Independent export generation and binding lifecycle do not need narrative revision; all historical versions need not load to edit current draft.
  - State/frame guard: Every complete narrative version and all reviews/approvals/nochange decisions/bindings/exports. Each edit appends complete immutable version. Sources512/periodChanges200 per version are invented caps; whole-lifetime lists unlimited. Correct runtime-Type JSON48KiB guard unaffected by CORE-01. Replay grows total narrative version payload plus independent exports.

## 80. SystemInstanceAccessReviewScope

- **Identity:** Stream uses instance id. DecisionId lookup returns prior success without comparing changed decision, reason, actor, effective dates or instance version.
- **Invariant:** Scope decisions for one instance advance a monotonic effective date and expected decision sequence with independent approver or exact-scope waiver.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Current decision and last effective date must remain ordered. Historical decisions are read history, not independent mutable owners. Moving the list to projection must retain the current/sequence gate.
- **Current observations:** ROOT-03 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Decide accepts live DeclaredSystemInstance and live SeparationOfDutiesWaiver and calls their behavior; even value ScopedSystemInstance overload still calls waiver.Allows. Owner should receive verified exact-scope value proof and enforce its own ordering/independence.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Current decision and last effective date must remain ordered. Historical decisions are read history, not independent mutable owners. Moving the list to projection must retain the current/sequence gate.
  - State/frame guard: All historical decisions retained in _decisions and EffectiveAt scans history; full replay and list grow for instance lifetime.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `DecideAccessReviewScope` → DecideAccessReviewScopeHandler; targets `SystemInstanceAccessReviewScope`; scope `IRequest<AccessReviewScopeDecisionView>, IApplicationInventoryWriteRequest, IProgramManagementRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ApplicationInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Applications/ApplicationInventoryAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ApplicationId, SystemInstanceId, ExpectedSystemInstanceRevision, ExpectedDecisionCount, Decision, Reason, EffectiveFrom`.
- **Events emitted:** `AccessReviewScopeDecided`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“Scope decisions for one instance advance a monotonic effective date and expected decision sequence with independent approver or exact-scope waiver.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Current decision and last effective date must remain ordered. Historical decisions are read history, not independent mutable owners. Moving the list to projection must retain the current/sequence gate.
  - Proof/authorization test: Decide accepts live DeclaredSystemInstance and live SeparationOfDutiesWaiver and calls their behavior; even value ScopedSystemInstance overload still calls waiver.Allows. Owner should receive verified exact-scope value proof and enforce its own ordering/independence.
  - Growth test: All historical decisions retained in _decisions and EffectiveAt scans history; full replay and list grow for instance lifetime.
- **Existing test references:** [AccessReviewScopeDecisionTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/Applications/AccessReviewScopeDecisionTests.cs), [AccessReviewScopeLegacyAndProjectionTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/Applications/AccessReviewScopeLegacyAndProjectionTests.cs), [PersonalAccessReviewScopeTransportTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/Applications/PersonalAccessReviewScopeTransportTests.cs)

## 81. Team

- **Identity:** Caller stable teamId in tenant stream; Define unconditional existing no-op hides changed intent. Expected-position OCC.
- **Invariant:** One team definition has an active/deleted lifecycle and built-in team cannot be deleted.
- **Boundary:** retain. Membership and role assignments need independent lifecycles, which current design already supplies; team itself owns only existence/deletion.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-09 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: No aggregate reads another; built-in identity value determines protection.
  - State/frame guard: Two flags; at most definition+deletion naturally; no membership roster retained.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `DefineTeam` → DefineTeamHandler; targets `Team`; scope `IClientRbacMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientRbacMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientRbacMutationAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, TeamId, Name`.
  - `DeleteTeam` → DeleteTeamHandler; targets `Team`; scope `IClientRbacMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientRbacMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientRbacMutationAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, TeamId`.
- **Events emitted:** `TeamDefined`, `TeamDeleted`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“One team definition has an active/deleted lifecycle and built-in team cannot be deleted.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Membership and role assignments need independent lifecycles, which current design already supplies; team itself owns only existence/deletion.
  - Proof/authorization test: No aggregate reads another; built-in identity value determines protection.
  - Growth test: Two flags; at most definition+deletion naturally; no membership roster retained.

## 82. TeamMember

- **Identity:** UUIDv5 tenant/team/member. Assignment can change episode; old RequestId not recognized; repeated Remove notFound; expected-position OCC.
- **Invariant:** One tenant/team/member relationship is active only for one current membership episode.
- **Boundary:** retain. Membership episode and active flag must be together; team-wide roster does not need an atomic revision. Tenant-manager invariant requires separate authoritative removal coordination.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-01 (pending), ACCESS-08 (in_progress)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Handler loads member at admission and rechecks episode after write; no aggregate-to-aggregate dependency. Manager hold restoration race remains.
  - State/frame guard: Assigned+episode scalar; history unbounded across rejoining cycles.

  - `AssignTeamMember` → AssignTeamMemberHandler; targets `AccessGrant, TeamMember, TenantManagerGuard`; scope `IClientRbacMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientRbacMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientRbacMutationAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, TeamId, MemberId`.
    - Helper/coordination: `TenantManagerInvariant, MemberAuthorityCleanup`. Guards: Authorize before resolving exact retries; use the request authorization scope IClientRbacMutationRequest. ## 83. TeamRole

- **Identity:** UUIDv5 tenant/team/role; immediate Assign retry no-op, older replay after Remove reassigns; repeated Remove notFound; append OCC.
- **Invariant:** One tenant/team/role relationship has a single active assignment.
- **Boundary:** retain. Each role assignment lifecycle independent; Team need not own collection. Critical built-in role removal policy remains external command admission.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-08 (in_progress)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.

  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: No live dependencies; external management authorizer protects built-in policy.
  - State/frame guard: Assigned scalar; unbounded assignment/removal stream lifetime.

  - `RemoveTeamRole` → RemoveTeamRoleHandler; targets `TeamRole`; scope `IClientRbacMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientRbacMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientRbacMutationAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, TeamId, RoleId, CommandId, AssignmentId`.
- **Events emitted:** `TeamRoleAssigned`, `TeamRoleRemoved`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“One tenant/team/role relationship has a single active assignment.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.

  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Each role assignment lifecycle independent; Team need not own collection. Critical built-in role removal policy remains external command admission.
  - Proof/authorization test: No live dependencies; external management authorizer protects built-in policy.

## 84. TechnologyComponent

- **Identity:** TechnologyInventoryStreams.Address(tenant,id) physically shared with InformationAsset/DataFlow; foreign-event sentinels reject other kinds and shared append OCC serializes competing creates. Cloud account id=systemInstanceId; other creation id=RequestId. Create compares current content instead of original; Revise expectedRevision lacks exact retry recognition.
- **Invariant:** One tenant inventory identity has exactly one record kind; a component keeps immutable category/system-instance binding, cloud account keyed to its unique instance, and category-specific valid content/lifecycle.
- **Boundary:** retain. Splitting class-specific record streams by type would lose shared identity-kind exclusion. Component mutable values/category restrictions belong one governed component lifecycle; no parent component roster needed.
- **Current observations:** ROOTTECH-02 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Handler validates workforce owner/cloud account reference at admission. Aggregate receives only content values; foreign events are same-stream kind exclusion, not live reads of another aggregate.
  - State/frame guard: Current content, own revision and kind-exclusion flags. No child history roster; repeated full revisions add unbounded lifetime replay despite scalar current state. No current lifetime/replay cost evidence supplied.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `RecordTechnologyComponent` → RecordTechnologyComponentHandler; targets `TechnologyComponent`; scope `IRequest<TechnologyComponentRegistration>, ITechnologyInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, Category, Name, OwnerPersonId`.
  - `ReviseTechnologyComponent` → ReviseTechnologyComponentHandler; targets `TechnologyComponent`; scope `ITechnologyInventoryWriteRequest`; authorizer `[TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ComponentId, ExpectedRevision, Name, OwnerPersonId, Lifecycle`.
- **Events emitted:** `TechnologyComponentRevisionRecorded`
- **Additional replay facts:** `DataFlowRevisionRecorded` [aggregate_fact], `InformationAssetRevisionRecorded` [aggregate_fact]
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“One tenant inventory identity has exactly one record kind; a component keeps immutable category/system-instance binding, cloud account keyed to its unique instance, and category-specific valid content/lifecycle.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Splitting class-specific record streams by type would lose shared identity-kind exclusion. Component mutable values/category restrictions belong one governed component lifecycle; no parent component roster needed.
  - Proof/authorization test: Handler validates workforce owner/cloud account reference at admission. Aggregate receives only content values; foreign events are same-stream kind exclusion, not live reads of another aggregate.
  - Growth test: Current content, own revision and kind-exclusion flags. No child history roster; repeated full revisions add unbounded lifetime replay despite scalar current state. No current lifetime/replay cost evidence supplied.
- **Existing test references:** [InventoryManagementIndependenceTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/AccessControl/InventoryManagementIndependenceTests.cs), [TechnologyComponentTypeIBaselineSourceContributorTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/Examinations/TechnologyComponentTypeIBaselineSourceContributorTests.cs), [InventoryBoundaryReferenceReadTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/TechnologyInventory/InventoryBoundaryReferenceReadTests.cs), [TechnologyInventoryAggregateTests.cs](../../../../test/Compliance.Tests/Features/TechnologyInventory/TechnologyInventoryAggregateTests.cs)

## 85. TemplateApplication

- **Identity:** Tenant / template-applications / UUIDv5(programId, templateId+trimmed ApplicationKey); per-entry planned UUIDv5(applicationId,entryKey). Same application/proposal/disposition compares generated timestamps; drafts have stable exact receipt comparison. Pinned Portia uses full physical stream replay and expected physical position; no durable request dedup is supplied.
- **Invariant:** One application pins exact published template provenance, fixed planned tenant draft identities and completion state; one pending later-version adoption proposal receives one immutable disposition before next successor.
- **Boundary:** process. All-drafts-created is technical orchestration; each draft has own lifecycle in its real owner and needs no lifetime copy in parent domain state. Pending successor/version ordering requires compact admission owner if decisions split.
- **Current observations:** ROOTASSURE-03 (pending), ROOTASSURE-04 (pending), CORE-01 (complete)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Published version VIEW is value proof; handler hydrates template/program admission. No live aggregate read. Current tree has no runtime RecordDraftCreated caller/reactor beyond method and tests; do not claim downstream execution is delivered.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: All-drafts-created is technical orchestration; each draft has own lifecycle in its real owner and needs no lifetime copy in parent domain state. Pending successor/version ordering requires compact admission owner if decisions split.
  - State/frame guard: Full source version with all content, fixed draft receipts and every successor version/proposal/decision retained and copied into every event. MaximumSuccessorDecisions64 forbids later valid successor. Nominal256KiB guard ineffective per CORE-01.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `ApplyMethodologyTemplate` → ApplyMethodologyTemplateHandler; targets `TemplateApplication`; scope `IProgramScopedRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProgramId, TemplateId, TemplateVersion, ApplicationKey`.

  - `RecordTemplateSuccessorDisposition` → RecordTemplateSuccessorDispositionHandler; targets `TemplateApplication`; scope `IProgramScopedRequest, IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs), [ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Programs/ProgramManagementAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProgramId, TemplateId, ApplicationKey, ExpectedRevision, NewTemplateVersion, Disposition, Rationale`.

- **Additional replay facts:** none.

  - Given the owner invariant (“One application pins exact published template provenance, fixed planned tenant draft identities and completion state; one pending later-version adoption proposal receives one immutable disposition before next successor.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: All-drafts-created is technical orchestration; each draft has own lifecycle in its real owner and needs no lifetime copy in parent domain state. Pending successor/version ordering requires compact admission owner if decisions split.
  - Proof/authorization test: Published version VIEW is value proof; handler hydrates template/program admission. No live aggregate read. Current tree has no runtime RecordDraftCreated caller/reactor beyond method and tests; do not claim downstream execution is delivered.

## 86. Tenant

- **Identity:** Create context.RequestId; Register exact retry currently conflict. Tenant commands mostly current-state idempotent; no stable per-transition intent identity. Append OCC.
- **Invariant:** One tenant lifecycle activates only after its slug and first-administrator conditions; offboarding forbids new active service; one current slug transition admitted at a time.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Tenant lifecycle and activation/current-slug link need coherence; global slug uniqueness belongs slug authority. Previous slug history and transport correlation need not live in tenant.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-10 (pending), ACCESS-16 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: No aggregate dependencies; handler/reactors coordinate slug/member sources.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Tenant lifecycle and activation/current-slug link need coherence; global slug uniqueness belongs slug authority. Previous slug history and transport correlation need not live in tenant.
  - State/frame guard: Current lifecycle, pending slug, requester, owner and previousSlugs history. Current memory grows with rename history; lifetime events also grow suspend/rename cycles.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `ActivateTenant` → ActivateTenantHandler; targets `Tenant`; scope `feature authorizer not explicit in interface`; authorizer `[ActivateTenantAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/ActivateTenantAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, FirstAdministratorUserId`.

## 87. TenantIdentityProvider

- **Identity:** Tenant+providerConnectionId; expectedRevision. TestKey/pending retry and result intent checks exist; Configure after revisions and older rotation retries not uniformly recognized.
- **Invariant:** Connection enables only with successful test of current credential epoch; rotation invalidates pending/old result; disabled connection stays terminal.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Credential epoch and activation decision must remain authoritative together. Test attempt lifecycle/history can be separate via explicit admission/epoch proof.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-07 (pending), ACCESS-10 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: No live aggregate dependency; handler supplies actor and effect result with validated provenance.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Credential epoch and activation decision must remain authoritative together. Test attempt lifecycle/history can be separate via explicit admission/epoch proof.
  - State/frame guard: Full configuration public view, all test history, pending request, latest secret ref. History repeated in every event;100 tests and100 credential revisions lifetime caps.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `ConfigureTenantIdentityProvider` → ConfigureTenantIdentityProviderHandler; targets `TenantIdentityProvider`; scope `IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProviderConnectionId, Issuer, ClientId, EncryptedCredentialReference, AllowedAudiences`.
  - `DisableTenantIdentityProvider` → DisableTenantIdentityProviderHandler; targets `TenantIdentityProvider`; scope `IClientRbacMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientRbacMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientRbacMutationAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProviderConnectionId, ExpectedRevision, Rationale`.
  - `RecordTenantIdentityProviderTestResult` → RecordTenantIdentityProviderTestResultHandler; targets `TenantIdentityProvider`; scope `feature authorizer not explicit in interface`; authorizer `[RecordTenantIdentityProviderTestResultAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/UserIdentities/RecordTenantIdentityProviderTestResultAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProviderConnectionId, ExpectedRevision, TestRequestEventId, Result, CompletedAt`.
  - `RotateTenantIdentityProviderCredentials` → RotateTenantIdentityProviderCredentialsHandler; targets `TenantIdentityProvider`; scope `IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProviderConnectionId, EncryptedCredentialReference, RotationReference`.
  - `TestTenantIdentityProvider` → TestTenantIdentityProviderHandler; targets `TenantIdentityProvider`; scope `IClientManagementMutationRequest`; authorizer `[ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/AccessControl/ClientManagementMutationAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, ProviderConnectionId, ExpectedRevision, TestKey`.
- **Events emitted:** `TenantIdentityProviderConfigured`, `TenantIdentityProviderCredentialsRotated`, `TenantIdentityProviderDisabled`, `TenantIdentityProviderTestRequested`, `TenantIdentityProviderTested`
- **Additional replay facts:** `TenantIdentityProviderTestedV2` [legacy_replay]
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**

## 88. TenantInvitation

- **Identity:** Lifetime UUIDv5 tenant+email; attempt random per invocation, accepted forever. Immediate acceptance same-user no-op; pending Invite always new fact. OCC expected-position.
- **Invariant:** One invitation offers immutable affiliation/admin purpose and exact scopes/expiry, with one current token and at most one accepted user.
- **Boundary:** split. All offered terms and accepted token must stay one lifecycle; transport status does not. Mailbox current invitation policy needs narrow coordination if invitations become independently identified.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-11 (pending), ACCESS-13 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Program existence is admission check; offered IDs are values, no live aggregate access. Reactors provision separately and member episode gate should preserve authority.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: All offered terms and accepted token must stay one lifecycle; transport status does not. Mailbox current invitation policy needs narrow coordination if invitations become independently identified.
  - State/frame guard: Current terms+guest program collection+token/delivery/accepted flags; pending reissue history unlimited. Guest scopes are complete invitation offer, not child program state.

## 89. TenantManagerGuard

- **Identity:** Tenant singleton, observed Sequence value, reasons per member, append OCC. Reasons suppress immediate duplicate withdrawal but no operation/generation token binds source write or restoration.
- **Invariant:** Removal from effective administrator set must leave another active tenant administrator.
- **Boundary:** retain. Without a shared authoritative decision/hold two removals could each rely on the other. The coordination boundary is justified; guard-to-member transition protocol is incomplete.

## 90. TenantOwner

- **Identity:** UUIDv5 tenant+user gives stable pair; Register flag no-ops duplicates; append OCC.
- **Invariant:** Only suppresses emitting the tenant/user relationship twice; no independent ownership choice is made.
- **Boundary:** project. Nothing domain-atomic breaks if this disappears: TenantRegistered already records the owner; relation/discovery is projection.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-15 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Created by reaction from TenantRegistered, no other aggregate reads inside.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Nothing domain-atomic breaks if this disappears: TenantRegistered already records the owner; relation/discovery is projection.
  - State/frame guard: Single flag, at most one duplicate relationship event per pair. Natural per-pair event size bounded but that does not justify an aggregate.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `RegisterTenantOwner` → RegisterTenantOwnerHandler; targets `TenantOwner`; scope `ITenantLifecycleReactionRequest`; authorizer `[TenantLifecycleReactionAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/TenantLifecycleReactionAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, UserId`.
- **Events emitted:** `TenantOwnerRegistered`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“Only suppresses emitting the tenant/user relationship twice; no independent ownership choice is made.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Nothing domain-atomic breaks if this disappears: TenantRegistered already records the owner; relation/discovery is projection.
  - Proof/authorization test: Created by reaction from TenantRegistered, no other aggregate reads inside.
  - Growth test: Single flag, at most one duplicate relationship event per pair. Natural per-pair event size bounded but that does not justify an aggregate.

## 91. TenantSlug

- **Identity:** UUIDv5 normalized slug. Register current same owner emits duplicate fact; rejection emits reply receipts; expected-position OCC enforces competing claims.
- **Invariant:** One normalized global slug can belong to one tenant and once surrendered is permanently retired.
- **Boundary:** retain. Global claim/retirement must be atomic; tenant activation and reply transport need not be in same owner.
- **Current observations:** ACCESS-14 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: No live aggregate; tenant ID value. Consumer sends tenant result reactions.
  - State/frame guard: Owner+retired scalar; naturally claim/surrender max2 facts except unlimited duplicate positive/negative request outcomes which break bound.
- **Commands and handlers:**
  - `RegisterTenantSlug` → RegisterTenantSlugHandler; targets `TenantSlug`; scope `ITenantLifecycleReactionRequest`; authorizer `[TenantLifecycleReactionAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/TenantLifecycleReactionAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, Slug`.
  - `SurrenderTenantSlug` → SurrenderTenantSlugHandler; targets `TenantSlug`; scope `ITenantLifecycleReactionRequest`; authorizer `[TenantLifecycleReactionAuthorizer.cs](../../../../src/Compliance.Core/Features/Tenants/TenantLifecycleReactionAuthorizer.cs)`; typed contract fields `TenantId, Slug`.
- **Events emitted:** `TenantSlugRegistered`, `TenantSlugRegistrationRejected`, `TenantSlugSurrenderRejected`, `TenantSlugSurrendered`
- **Additional replay facts:** none.
- **Tests to lock the boundary:**
  - Given the owner invariant (“One normalized global slug can belong to one tenant and once surrendered is permanently retired.”), test allowed and rejected transitions, asserting rejected commands append no domain fact.
  - Test aggregate rehydration/replay after every accepted transition and after a later edit or lifecycle episode.
  - Test exact retry after later edits/removal/reinstatement; same identity with changed actor, kind, intent or expected revision conflicts without appending.
  - Test expected-revision races: concurrent writers cannot both advance the same owner decision.
  - Test retained-state and serialized-event limits with combined maximum fields, attribution and Unicode; no lifetime cap may stand in for an owner split.
  - Boundary test: Global claim/retirement must be atomic; tenant activation and reply transport need not be in same owner.
  - Proof/authorization test: No live aggregate; tenant ID value. Consumer sends tenant result reactions.
  - Growth test: Owner+retired scalar; naturally claim/surrender max2 facts except unlimited duplicate positive/negative request outcomes which break bound.

## 93. TypeIIObservationCalendar

- **Identity:** tenant/type-ii-observation-calendars/programId; stable periodId and exact opening intent. Amendment retries compare exact intent. Release of any already inactive period returns success without comparing closed snapshot, so changed release facts can be acknowledged as old success.
- **Invariant:** Active program Type II date ranges, including both current and reserved amendment ranges, must never overlap; a pending amendment must finish or cancel before date range release.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Splitting period reservations by period without a shared admission owner breaks non-overlap. Retain program-wide reservation owner; keep only active ranges/pending holds, project inactive histories. If sharding by date buckets becomes necessary reserve all intersecting buckets in canonical order and recover/release partial reservations.
- **Current observations:** OPS-09 (pending), OPS-10 (pending), OPS-16 (pending)
- **Guards:**
  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.
  - Use typed client operation identity for repeated mutations, then compare original actor, command kind, canonical intent, lifecycle episode and original expected revision.
  - Use owner-native facts for retry resolution; exact retries append nothing, changed intent conflicts, and new operations enforce expected revision.
  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Only scalar/value proofs from period handlers; no live aggregate. Hold-first start/amend/freeze ordering protects safety, but interrupted sequences have no autonomous recovery.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Splitting period reservations by period without a shared admission owner breaks non-overlap. Retain program-wide reservation owner; keep only active ranges/pending holds, project inactive histories. If sharding by date buckets becomes necessary reserve all intersecting buckets in canonical order and recover/release partial reservations.
  - State/frame guard: {"retained": "All ever reserved periods, including released ranges and last/pending amendment; complete current view copies all periods.", "lifetimeReplay": "Released entries never removed. MaximumPeriods10,000 prevents all future period reservations once lifetime count reached; replay also caps lifetime history."}

## 94. TypeIIPeriod

- **Identity:** tenant/type-ii-periods/periodId, supplied stable minted ID; Start compares exact plan/opening, but uses mutable current actor fields. Other commands vary: generationKey exact compares intent; approval/amendment recognize latest intent, close snapshots use stable IDs; no uniform historical request intent.
- **Invariant:** One period binds exact approved opening plan/population; close must reconcile the approved expected population, independently acknowledge all unresolved material items, and freeze the same approved sources. Close amendments preserve original expected population/acknowledgements and chain exact changed sources.
- **Boundary:** narrow. The immutable approved expected-population manifest is a legitimate value proof for close, not a live roster of occurrence children. Keep a narrow period close/lineage owner; move occurrence-generation process/batches and immutable close snapshots/history out of active state. Preserve canonical frozen manifest and current closure lineage digest so atomic close rules remain exact.
- **Current observations:** OPS-09 (pending), OPS-10 (pending), OPS-12 (pending), OPS-17 (pending), CORE-01 (complete)

  - Boundary/coordination guard: The immutable approved expected-population manifest is a legitimate value proof for close, not a live roster of occurrence children. Keep a narrow period close/lineage owner; move occurrence-generation process/batches and immutable close snapshots/history out of active state. Preserve canonical frozen manifest and current closure lineage digest so atomic close rules remain exact.

## 95. TypeIIPlan

- **Identity:** Tenant / type-ii-plans / planId; initial/decision intent compares; revision command fails stale retry before comparison; current decision immutable. Portia appends against the hydrated physical stream position; a logical revision does not create another physical boundary.
- **Invariant:** Only current proposed content with exact carried-gap acknowledgements receives an independent review; executive approval requires review and three distinct roles, after which plan is immutable.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Current authored content/reviewer/approver must remain one same-version decision. Past proposal bodies/reviews are historical records and need not remain state.
- **Current observations:** ASSURE-17 (pending), ASSURE-11 (pending), CORE-01 (complete)

  - Authenticate and authorize before retry resolution; scope the actor to this owner’s tenant/program and stream identity.

  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Exact source/control values prepared by handlers; aggregate never hydrates another owner.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Current authored content/reviewer/approver must remain one same-version decision. Past proposal bodies/reviews are historical records and need not remain state.
  - State/frame guard: All versions, review and approvals retained, max24 versions,384KiB full-state ceiling. Control/gap lists are complete current proposal value, not live control lifecycles.

## 96. UserIdentity

- **Identity:** UUIDv5 provider+subject; registration stable optional user binding; revoke retries exact replacement no-op, changed replacement conflict. OCC.
- **Invariant:** External provider+subject associates to exactly one platform user; revoked identity cannot authenticate and has one exact replacement.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Association/revocation must stay together. Provider presentation observations do not decide identity authority and can be separate observation/audit source.

## 98. WorkDigestDispatch

- **Identity:** tenant/work-digest-dispatches/memberId with dictionary weekOf children. Schedule rejects duplicates/future-known-week; processor deterministic messageId. Physical OCC claims prevent concurrent send. No-op bool does not compare changed schedule intent as conflict.
- **Invariant:** Technical dispatch invariant: one reservation/message per member-week, one in-flight send, retries only after definite transient rejection, and ambiguous external outcome freezes until authorized evidence-backed retry.
- **Boundary:** process. Split historical member roster into per-member-week dispatch process keyed by business delivery identity, retaining only narrow member scheduling cursor if monotonic week admission is required. Unknown-delivery holds and explicit evidence authorizations must remain durable; never replace with projection or blind retry.

- **Guards:**

  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: Processor reads preferences and content via service before attempt; aggregate accepts only value schedule and scalar result. No live aggregate.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Split historical member roster into per-member-week dispatch process keyed by business delivery identity, retaining only narrow member scheduling cursor if monotonic week admission is required. Unknown-delivery holds and explicit evidence authorizations must remain durable; never replace with projection or blind retry.
  - State/frame guard: {"retained": "Every weekly dispatch current state forever, including terminal statuses and evidence-backed unknown retry authorization.", "lifetimeReplay": "Each member adds one dictionary entry and several process events per week for lifetime; retry bounds are operational delivery policy, not invented business lifetime caps."}

## 99. WorkDigestPreference

- **Boundary:** retain. Retain per-member notification consent/time-zone authority or equivalent dedicated versioned consent owner. Matching Member identity does not justify a shared revision. Add logical retry and expected revision so an old consent command cannot override later consent.

## 100. WorkItemAssignment

- **Identity:** tenant/work-item-assignments/v5(programId,workItemId); expectedRevision protects per-item mutation and physical append. AssignTo/Escalate receive no request identity, so exact successful replay is stale/conflict; history import only empty target.
- **Invariant:** One source-derived work item has current assignee/escalation revision; escalation is accepted at most once, assignment commands require expected revision. Source eligibility and completion belong to producing workflow.
- **Boundary:** narrow. Per-item boundary is correct; different items/programs already isolate streams. Keep current assignee/escalated and required assignment action guards; move full history to projection and legacy import to process storage while preserving attribution.
- **Current observations:** OPS-12 (pending), OPS-13 (pending), OPS-15 (pending)

  - Keep validation, uniqueness, status, approval and segregation rules in this aggregate’s atomic command boundary; reject without appending on failure.
  - Cross-owner guard: WorkQueueReader hydrates assignment source, so current assignment check is not merely an eventually-consistent projection. Handlers check producer eligibility/SoD at admission and pass scalar target; no live aggregate in assignment.
  - Boundary/coordination guard: Per-item boundary is correct; different items/programs already isolate streams. Keep current assignee/escalated and required assignment action guards; move full history to projection and legacy import to process storage while preserving attribution.
  - State/frame guard: {"retained": "Current assignee/escalation/revision plus complete assignment/escalation history.", "lifetimeReplay": "A long-lived source work item can be delegated/reassigned without limit, and all history retained/replayed. ## 101. WorkRelationship
